```python
import numpy as np
import math
import time


def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr) * dx)


def enforce_sum(h, target):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(200):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = float(target) - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            idx = int(np.argmax(room))
            add = min(float(room[idx]), diff)
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(float(h[idx]), -diff)
            h[idx] -= sub

    s = float(np.sum(h))
    diff = float(target) - s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < (1.0 - 1e-12)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-12
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def initial_d(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    D = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    if n == 0:
        return D
    off = n - 1
    omh = 1.0 - h
    D[off] = float(np.dot(h, omh))
    for t in range(1, n):
        D[off + t] = float(np.dot(h[:n - t], omh[t:]))
        D[off - t] = float(np.dot(h[t:], omh[:n - t]))
    return D


def get_max_info(D):
    m = float(np.max(D))
    tol = 1e-10
    cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(D >= m - tol))
    return m, cnt


def coordinate_change(h, D, p, delta):
    n = int(h.size)
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    off = n - 1
    src = off - p
    tgt = p
    D[src:src + n] += delta * (1.0 - h)
    D[tgt:tgt + n] -= delta * h[::-1]
    D[off] -= delta * delta
    h[p] += delta


def apply_exchange(h, D, p, q, delta):
    if p == q or delta == 0.0:
        return
    coordinate_change(h, D, p, delta)
    coordinate_change(h, D, q, -delta)


def eval_exchange_revert(h, D, p, q, delta):
    if p == q or delta <= 1e-15:
        return None
    if h[p] + delta > 1.0 + 1e-10 or h[q] - delta < -1e-10:
        return None
    oldp = float(h[p])
    oldq = float(h[q])
    apply_exchange(h, D, p, q, delta)
    nm, nc = get_max_info(D)
    apply_exchange(h, D, q, p, delta)
    h[p] = oldp
    h[q] = oldq
    return nm, nc


def targeted_improve(h, D, n, cur_m, cur_cnt, rng, eval_limit=150, binary_only=False):
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    D[:] = initial_d(h)
    cur_m, cur_cnt = get_max_info(D)

    off = n - 1
    maxD = float(np.max(D))
    near = np.where(D >= maxD - 1e-10)[0]
    if near.size == 0:
        return cur_m, cur_cnt, False

    if near.size > 3:
        wlist = rng.choice(near, 3, replace=False)
    else:
        wlist = near

    best_nm = cur_m
    best_nc = cur_cnt
    best_p = -1
    best_q = -1
    best_delta = 0.0
    found = False
    evaluated = 0

    if binary_only:
        us = (1.0,)
    else:
        us = (1.0, 0.5, 0.1)

    for w in wlist:
        if evaluated >= eval_limit:
            break
        t = int(w - off)

        if t > 0:
            low = 0
            high = n - t
            if high <= low:
                continue
            for _ in range(60):
                if evaluated >= eval_limit:
                    break
                i = int(rng.randint(low, high))
                contrib = h[i] * (1.0 - h[i + t])
                if contrib > 1e-8:
                    p = i + t
                    q = i
                    dmax = min(float(h[q]), float(1.0 - h[p]))
                    if dmax > 1e-9:
                        for u in us:
                            delta = dmax * u
                            info = eval_exchange_revert(h, D, p, q, delta)
                            if info is not None:
                                evaluated += 1
                                nm, nc = info
                                if nm < best_nm - 1e-10 or (abs(nm - best_nm) <= 1e-10 and nc < best_nc):
                                    best_nm = nm
                                    best_nc = nc
                                    best_p = p
                                    best_q = q
                                    best_delta = delta
                                    found = True
                            if evaluated >= eval_limit:
                                break
                            if evaluated % 30 == 0:
                                np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
                                D[:] = initial_d(h)
        elif t < 0:
            s = -t
            low = s
            high = n
            if high <= low:
                continue
            for _ in range(60):
                if evaluated >= eval_limit:
                    break
                i = int(rng.randint(low, high))
                contrib = h[i] * (1.0 - h[i - s])
                if contrib > 1e-8:
                    p = i - s
                    q = i
                    dmax = min(float(h[q]), float(1.0 - h[p]))
                    if dmax > 1e-9:
                        for u in us:
                            delta = dmax * u
                            info = eval_exchange_revert(h, D, p, q, delta)
                            if info is not None:
                                evaluated += 1
                                nm, nc = info
                                if nm < best_nm - 1e-10 or (abs(nm - best_nm) <= 1e-10 and nc < best_nc):
                                    best_nm = nm
                                    best_nc = nc
                                    best_p = p
                                    best_q = q
                                    best_delta = delta
                                    found = True
                            if evaluated >= eval_limit:
                                break
                            if evaluated % 30 == 0:
                                np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
                                D[:] = initial_d(h)
        else:
            for _ in range(60):
                if evaluated >= eval_limit:
                    break
                p = int(rng.randint(n))
                q = int(rng.randint(n))
                if p == q:
                    continue
                if h[p] < 0.5 and h[q] > 0.5:
                    dmax = min(1.0 - h[p], h[q])
                elif h[p] > 0.5 and h[q] < 0.5:
                    p2, q2 = q, p
                    dmax = min(1.0 - h[p2], h[q2])
                    p, q = p2, q2
                else:
                    continue
                if dmax > 1e-9:
                    for u in us:
                        delta = dmax * u
                        info = eval_exchange_revert(h, D, p, q, delta)
                        if info is not None:
                            evaluated += 1
                            nm, nc = info
                            if nm < best_nm - 1e-10 or (abs(nm - best_nm) <= 1e-10 and nc < best_nc):
                                best_nm = nm
                                best_nc = nc
                                best_p = p
                                best_q = q
                                best_delta = delta
                                found = True
                        if evaluated >= eval_limit:
                            break
                        if evaluated % 30 == 0:
                            np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
                            D[:] = initial_d(h)

    if found:
        apply_exchange(h, D, best_p, best_q, best_delta)

    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    D[:] = initial_d(h)
    nm, nc = get_max_info(D)
    return nm, nc, found


def thue_morse_h(n):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        if bin(i).count("1") % 2 == 0:
            h[i] = 1.0
    return h


def random_binary(n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    target = n // 2
    if target > 0 and n > 0:
        idx = rng.choice(n, target, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def resample_values(vals, n):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    m = int(vals.size)
    if m == n:
        return vals.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(vals[0]), dtype=float)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, vals)


def threshold_top_half(vals, n, rng=None, noise=0.0):
    v = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float).copy()
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    if noise > 0.0 and rng is not None:
        v = v + rng.normal(0.0, noise, v.size)
    target = n // 2
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target <= 0:
        return h
    if target >= n:
        h[:] = 1.0
        return h
    order = np.argsort(v, kind="stable")[::-1]
    idx = order[:target]
    h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def antisym_from_first(first):
    first = np.asarray(first, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    return np.concatenate((first, 1.0 - first))


def optimize_state(h_init, rng, timeout):
    h = np.asarray(h_init, dtype=float).copy()
    n = int(h.size)
    h = enforce_sum(h, n / 2.0)
    D = initial_d(h)
    cur_m, cur_cnt = get_max_info(D)

    best_m = cur_m
    best_h = h.copy()

    start = time.time()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(timeout))

    # Fast binary swap phase for fermion/binary-like starts.
    hr = np.round(h)
    if float(np.max(np.abs(h - hr))) < 1e-7:
        h = hr
        D = initial_d(h)
        cur_m, cur_cnt = get_max_info(D)
        best_m = cur_m
        best_h = h.copy()

        zeros = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] < 0.5]
        ones = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] >= 0.5]

        T = 1.5
        no_improve = 0
        trial = 0
        next_check = 2000

        while True:
            if trial == next_check:
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break
                next_check = trial + 2000

            if not zeros or not ones:
                break

            pi = rng.randint(len(zeros))
            oi = rng.randint(len(ones))
            p = zeros[pi]
            q = ones[oi]

            apply_exchange(h, D, p, q, 1.0)
            nm, nc = get_max_info(D)
            diff = nm - cur_m

            accept = False
            improved = False
            if diff < -1e-12:
                accept = True
                improved = True
            elif abs(diff) <= 1e-12:
                if nc < cur_cnt:
                    accept = True
                    improved = True
                elif rng.random() < 0.02:
                    accept = True
            else:
                if diff <= 4.0:
                    prob = math.exp(-diff / max(T, 1e-12))
                    if rng.random() < prob:
                        accept = True

            if accept:
                cur_m = nm
                cur_cnt = nc
                if nm < best_m - 1e-12:
                    best_m = nm
                    best_h = h.copy()
                zeros[pi] = q
                ones[oi] = p
                if improved:
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
            else:
                apply_exchange(h, D, q, p, 1.0)
                no_improve += 1

            trial += 1

            if trial % 1000 == 0:
                T = max(0.05, T * 0.99)

                if no_improve > 8000 and time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
                    newm, newc, acc = targeted_improve(
                        h, D, n, cur_m, cur_cnt, rng,
                        eval_limit=200, binary_only=True
                    )
                    if acc:
                        cur_m, cur_cnt = newm, newc
                        if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                            best_m = cur_m
                            best_h = h.copy()
                        zeros = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] < 0.5]
                        ones = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] >= 0.5]
                    no_improve = 0

                if no_improve > 50000:
                    T = min(3.0, T + 1.0)
                    no_improve = 0

        if time.time() < deadline - 15.0:
            h = best_h.copy()
            D = initial_d(h)
            cur_m = best_m
            cur_cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(D >= cur_m - 1e-10))

    # Continuous polish / fractional search phase.
    if time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
        h = best_h.copy()
        D = initial_d(h)
        cur_m = best_m
        cur_cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(D >= cur_m - 1e-10))

        T_big = 0.5
        T_small = 0.03
        no_improve = 0
        trial = 0
        next_check = 2000

        inc = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-9]
        dec = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] > 1e-9]

        while True:
            if trial == next_check:
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break
                next_check = trial + 2000

            if not inc or not dec:
                inc = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-9]
                dec = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] > 1e-9]
                if not inc or not dec:
                    break

            if trial % 3000 == 0:
                inc = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-9]
                dec = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] > 1e-9]
                if not inc or not dec:
                    break

            p = inc[rng.randint(len(inc))]
            q = dec[rng.randint(len(dec))]

            if p != q:
                maxd = min(1.0 - h[p], h[q])
                if maxd > 1e-10:
                    r = rng.random()
                    if r < 0.35:
                        delta = maxd
                    elif r < 0.75:
                        delta = maxd * rng.uniform(0.05, 0.5)
                    else:
                        delta = maxd * rng.uniform(0.005, 0.05)

                    if delta > 1e-9:
                        oldp = float(h[p])
                        oldq = float(h[q])
                        apply_exchange(h, D, p, q, delta)

                        nm, nc = get_max_info(D)
                        diff = nm - cur_m

                        accept = False
                        improved = False
                        if diff < -1e-10:
                            accept = True
                            improved = True
                        elif abs(diff) <= 1e-10:
                            if nc < cur_cnt:
                                accept = True
                                improved = True
                            elif rng.random() < 0.05:
                                accept = True
                        else:
                            if delta >= 0.5:
                                if diff <= 1.0 and rng.random() < math.exp(-diff / max(T_big, 1e-12)):
                                    accept = True
                            else:
                                if diff <= 0.25 and rng.random() < math.exp(-diff / max(T_small, 1e-12)):
                                    accept = True

                        if accept:
                            cur_m = nm
                            cur_cnt = nc
                            if nm < best_m - 1e-12:
                                best_m = nm
                                best_h = h.copy()
                            if improved:
                                no_improve = 0
                            else:
                                no_improve += 1
                        else:
                            apply_exchange(h, D, q, p, delta)
                            h[p] = oldp
                            h[q] = oldq
                            no_improve += 1

            trial += 1

            if trial % 2000 == 0:
                T_big = max(0.05, T_big * 0.99)
                T_small = max(0.001, T_small * 0.99)

                if no_improve > 8000 and time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
                    newm, newc, acc = targeted_improve(
                        h, D, n, cur_m, cur_cnt, rng,
                        eval_limit=150, binary_only=False
                    )
                    if acc:
                        cur_m, cur_cnt = newm, newc
                        if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                            best_m = cur_m
                            best_h = h.copy()
                    no_improve = 0

                if cur_m > best_m + 2.0 and no_improve > 50000 and time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    D = initial_d(h)
                    cur_m = best_m
                    cur_cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(D >= cur_m - 1e-10))
                    inc = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-9]
                    dec = [i for i in range(n) if h[i] > 1e-9]
                    no_improve = 0
                    trial = 0
                    next_check = 2000

                if trial % 50000 == 0:
                    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
                    D[:] = initial_d(h)
                    cur_m, cur_cnt = get_max_info(D)

    return best_h, best_m


def process_tasks(tasks, best_h, best_c5, best_n, rng, start_time, budget_s):
    reserve = 10.0

    for task in tasks:
        n, h_init, timeout = task
        rem = float(budget_s) - (time.time() - start_time)
        if rem < 20.0:
            break

        timeout = min(float(timeout), rem - reserve)
        if timeout < 10.0:
            break

        h_opt, m_opt = optimize_state(h_init, rng, timeout)

        h_cand = enforce_sum(h_opt, n / 2.0)
        c_cand = compute_c5(h_cand)

        hr = np.round(h_opt)
        if float(np.max(np.abs(h_opt - hr))) < 0.1:
            h_r = enforce_sum(hr, n / 2.0)
            c_r = compute_c5(h_r)
            if c_r < c_cand:
                h_cand = h_r
                c_cand = c_r

        if c_cand < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_h = h_cand.copy()
            best_c5 = float(c_cand)
            best_n = int(n)

        rem = float(budget_s) - (time.time() - start_time)
        if rem < 20.0:
            break

    return best_h, best_c5, best_n


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    np.random.seed(seed)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    start_time = time.time()

    best_h = None
    best_c5 = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    parent = None
    try:
        pv = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if pv.size > 0:
            parent = pv.copy()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        hp = enforce_sum(parent, parent.size / 2.0)
        c = compute_c5(hp)
        best_h = hp
        best_c5 = c
        best_n = int(parent.size)

    # Resolution 256: cheap, and can give very sharp binary/fractional improvements.
    tasks = []
    n = 256
    if parent is not None:
        hp256 = resample_values(parent, n)
        tasks.append((n, threshold_top_half(hp256, n, rng, 0.0), 150))
        tasks.append((n, enforce_sum(hp256, n / 2.0), 100))
        tasks.append((n, threshold_top_half(hp256, n, rng, 0.03), 60))
    else:
        tasks.append((n, random_binary(n, rng), 150))

    tasks.append((n, thue_morse_h(n), 80))
    tasks.append((n, antisym_from_first(thue_morse_h(n // 2)), 30))
    tasks.append((n, random_binary(n, rng), 50))
    tasks.append((n, random_binary(n, rng), 40))

    best_h, best_c5, best_n = process_tasks(
        tasks, best_h, best_c5, best_n, rng, start_time, budget_s
    )

    # Resolution 512: granularity 2/512 = 0.00390625, allowing values below 0.38085.
    rem = float(budget_s) - (time.time() - start_time)
    if rem > 250.0:
        tasks = []
        n = 512

        if parent is not None:
            hp512 = resample_values(parent, n)
            tasks.append((n, threshold_top_half(hp512, n, rng, 0.0), 150))
            tasks.append((n, enforce_sum(hp512, n / 2.0), 100))
            tasks.append((n, threshold_top_half(hp512, n, rng, 0.03), 50))
        else:
            tasks.append((n, random_binary(n, rng), 150))

        if best_h is not None:
            bb = resample_values(best_h, n)
            tasks.append((n, threshold_top_half(bb, n, rng, 0.0), 110))
            tasks.append((n, enforce_sum(bb, n / 2.0), 50))

        tasks.append((n, thue_morse_h(n), 70))
        tasks.append((n, random_binary(n, rng), 60))

        best_h, best_c5, best_n = process_tasks(
            tasks, best_h, best_c5, best_n, rng, start_time, budget_s
        )

    # Resolution 1024: if time remains, try a finer scale.
    rem = float(budget_s) - (time.time() - start_time)
    if rem > 150.0:
        tasks = []
        n = 1024

        if best_h is not None:
            bb = resample_values(best_h, n)
            tasks.append((n, threshold_top_half(bb, n, rng, 0.0), 90))
        else:
            tasks.append((n, random_binary(n, rng), 90))

        tasks.append((n, thue_morse_h(n), 40))

        best_h, best_c5, best_n = process_tasks(
            tasks, best_h, best_c5, best_n, rng, start_time, budget_s
        )

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = random_binary(256, rng)
        best_n = 256

    best_h = enforce_sum(best_h, best_n / 2.0)
    best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    return best_h, best_c5, int(best_n)
```
